&lt;project_context>
# 项目: gen-depth-contamination

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: cqa1-22115
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh cqa1-22115  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：Qwen-cont C4 增量样本实验（10k 总样本收窄 CI）

**目的**：Qwen-cont 1v2 Bonferroni 99.5% CI lower bound=0.5976 < θ=0.60。增加样本到 10k 缩小 boot_std，尝试恢复 CI lower > θ。

### 服务器信息
- SSH 别名：cqa1-22115（端口 31514）
- 项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination
- GPU：cuda:1
- 环境：source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base

### 实验参数
- **模型**: Qwen2.5-1.5B（base，continuation mode）— 注意是 base 不是 Instruct
- **域**: C4
- **Scorer**: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct
- **当前样本量**: 5,000（原始 exp）
- **目标样本量**: 10,000

### Step 1: 查找原始实验数据和模型路径

```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination
# 找 Qwen-cont C4 的原始数据
find data -name "*.jsonl" -path "*qwen*cont*" -o -name "*.jsonl" -path "*qwen_base*" -o -name "*.jsonl" -path "*exp016*" | head -10
ls data/data_exp016_qwen_base/ 2>/dev/null
# Qwen base 模型路径
find /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache -name "*Qwen2.5-1.5B" -not -name "*Instruct*" -type d | head -3
# Qwen Instruct (scorer) 路径
ls /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 2>/dev/null || ls /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-1___5B 2>/dev/null
```

### Step 2: 确定增量 vs 全量策略

检查 run_pipeline.py 是否支持 append/增量：
```bash
python3 scripts/run_pipeline.py --help 2>&1 | grep -i "append\|resume\|start_sample\|offset"
```

**如果支持增量**：用 seed=43 额外生成 5000 samples，然后合并
**如果不支持**：从头生成 10,000 samples（seed=42），全流程重跑

### Step 3: 准备数据目录
```bash
mkdir -p data/exp_qwen_cont_10k results/exp_qwen_cont_10k
# 复制 C4 depth_0 种子数据
# 如果需要 10k samples 的 depth_0，可能需要从更大的 C4 池采样
wc -l data/data_exp016_qwen_base/depth_0.jsonl 2>/dev/null
# 如果只有 5000 行，需要扩展到 10000
```

**如果 depth_0 只有 5000 行**：需要从 C4 数据源额外采样 5000 行。检查：
```bash
find /root/autodl-tmp -name "c4*" -o -name "C4*" | head -10
python3 -c "from datasets import load_dataset; ds = load_dataset('c4', 'en', split='train', streaming=True); print(next(iter(ds)))" 2>/dev/null || echo "NO_C4_DATASET"
```

### Step 4: 构建命令并通过 registry + submit_training_job 提交

先更新 registry：
```python
update_registry(exp_id="exp_qwen_cont_10k", field="hypothesis", value="增加 Qwen-cont C4 样本量从 5k→10k 可将 1v2 bootstrap std 缩小 ~√2 倍，使 Bonferroni 99.5% CI lower bound 恢复到 θ=0.60 以上", project="gen-depth-contamination")
# 然后推进状态 PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED
```

Pipeline 命令模板（两阶段，Qwen base 生成 + Qwen Instruct scoring）：
```bash
bash -c "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination && python3 scripts/run_pipeline.py \
  --model_path &lt;qwen_base_path> \
  --data_dir data/exp_qwen_cont_10k \
  --results_dir results/exp_qwen_cont_10k \
  --generation_mode continuation \
  --top_p 0.95 --temperature 1.0 \
  --seed 42 --num_samples 10000 --max_depth 5 --batch_size 16 \
  && rm -f data/exp_qwen_cont_10k/features.csv \
  && python3 scripts/run_pipeline.py \
  --model_path &lt;qwen_instruct_path> \
  --data_dir data/exp_qwen_cont_10k \
  --results_dir results/exp_qwen_cont_10k \
  --skip_generation \
  --seed 42 --num_samples 10000 --max_depth 5"
```

submit_training_job 用 cuda:1, timeout 6h。

### Step 5: 提交后验证进程启动
```bash
# 检查 tmux session
tmux ls | grep qwen_cont_10k
# 检查 GPU 占用
nvidia-smi | grep -A2 "cuda:1\|MiB"
```

### 输出要求
1. 原始数据路径和模型路径
2. 选择的策略（增量 vs 全量）及原因
3. depth_0 样本数是否足够 10k
4. submit_training_job 返回的 PID
5. 如果 depth_0 不够 10k，报告此问题和解决方案

The pipeline automatically downloads C4 data and creates depth_0.jsonl with `num_samples` samples. The check at line 74-75 shows that if depth_0.jsonl already exists and has enough samples, it skips the download. Since we're creating a new data directory `data/exp_qwen_cont_10k`, the pipeline will auto-download 10,000 samples from C4.

Now I need to understand the full flow:
1. Pipeline with base model (generation_mode=continuation) generates depth_0-5 with 10k samples


2. Then run the scorer (Qwen Instruct) with --skip_generation to do feature extraction and analysis

Let me check what the pipeline actually does when running with or without --skip_generation to understand if there's a generation step followed by a separate scoring step.

Now I understand the full pipeline flow. When we use `--skip_generation`, it still loads the model and runs feature extraction + analysis. So the two-phase approach is:

1. Phase 1: Run with base model (generate + extract features) - but we'll delete features.csv after
2. Phase 2: Run with Instruct model + --skip_generation (re-extract features with Instruct model + analysis)

But actually, Phase 1 will also run feature extraction and analysis (steps 4-5). These will use the base model. Then in Phase 2, we delete features.csv and run again with Instruct model for feature extraction.


The feature extraction function checks if features.csv already exists and skips if it does, which is why we need to delete it between phases. So the workflow is: generate depth files and features with the base model, then delete features.csv and re-run with the Instruct model to regenerate features for scoring.

Now I'm setting up the command with `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1` to isolate to the second GPU, configuring the HuggingFace environment variables, and checking for any existing processes on that device before constructing the full command.
```
